Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The number_theory variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 72 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 115 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the number_theory decomposition. By minimality, the element a^72 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 115-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
